# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-21)

## Corpus Check
- 4572 files · ~10,675,062 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 105 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 34, .css 16, .entitlements 10)

## Summary
- 64542 nodes · 185953 edges · 1363 communities (1218 shown, 145 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7667 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `2f4999e1`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
